Use when the user asks to "spec out", "spec plan", "write requirements", "write a design doc", or plan a feature before implementing it. - **spec-00-scope-split**: 當使用者輸入 /spec-00 [path]、/spec-run（帶來新需求時），或說「評估需求大小」「這個需求要不要切」「幫我切分需求」「拆成幾個模組」時使用。在 spec-01 之前評估一份新需求的大小：未超過門檻就以單一模組繼續；超過門檻就自動決定切分方案並落盤，只有不確定之處會改變下一個要跑的模組範圍時才停下來問，其餘疑點放進待人檢視清單、不阻擋流程。之後 spec-01 一次只掃一個模組。 ([yi-luan/specengine-plugin](https://github.com/yi-luan/specengine-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/spec-00-scope-split) · ★11)
- **spec-009-d1-matematica**: Suite TDD para D1 (Raciocinio Matematico Formal) do CORA-Eval. 8 CTs: Pitagoras, Gauss, Fatorial, Fibonacci, Primos, MDC, Trigonometria, Derivada. Validacao via TDD com pytest. Use quando precisar validar raciocinio matematico formal do ecossistema OpenCode no dominio da matematica pura. ([MarceloClaro/OpenCode_Ecosystem](https://github.com/MarceloClaro/OpenCode_Ecosystem/tree/HEAD/skills/research/spec-009-d1-matematica) · ★12)
- **spec-01-discovery-bootstrap**: 當使用者輸入 /spec-01 [path]、/spec-run，或說「冷啟動」「掃需求」「幫我分析這個需求」時使用。對新需求靜態掃描：鎖定核心業務切片、抽取行為矩陣與 DBML 草稿、初始化術語表、套用既有組織慣例、以高風險探測清單找盲點並轉成 MCQ 佇列。純掃描器角色，唯一允許問的是「這屬於哪個模組」。新需求必須先經 spec-00 確認切分方案，本階段一次只掃方案中的一個模組。 ([yi-luan/specengine-plugin](https://github.com/yi-luan/specengine-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/spec-01-discovery-bootstrap) · ★11)

- **spec-012-d3-estatistica**: Suite TDD para D3 (Raciocinio Estatistico) do CORA-Eval. 9 testes em niveis N2-N3: Teste t (2), ANOVA, Regressao Linear, Correlacao de Pearson, Bootstrap, MCMC, PCA, Correcao Multiplas Comparacoes. 11 funcoes implementadas. Validacao via TDD com dados sinteticos seed 42. ([MarceloClaro/OpenCode_Ecosystem](https://github.com/MarceloClaro/OpenCode_Ecosystem/tree/HEAD/skills/research/spec-012-d3-estatistica) · ★12)
- **spec-013-d11-longhorizon**: Suite TDD para D11 (Raciocinio de Longo Horizonte em DAG) do CORA-Eval. 150 CTs em 5 dominios (Logica, Matematica, Planejamento, Fluxo de Codigo, Cientifico) x 4 niveis (N1-N4).
